package hu.peterkepkeret.ajanlatkeszito.torzsadat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.json.AutoConfigureJsonTesters;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.web.servlet.AutoConfigureMockMvc;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import org.springframework.boot.test.json.JacksonTester;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.empty;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.hasSize;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.is;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.not;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.notNullValue;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.put;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.content;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

@AutoConfigureMockMvc
@AutoConfigureJsonTesters
@SpringBootTest
@RequiredArgsConstructor(onConstructor_ = @Autowired)
@Transactional
public class TorzsadatControllerIT {

    private static final String BASE_URL = "/api/torzsadat";
    private static final String GET_TORZSADAT_URL = "/api/torzsadat/{id}";

    private final MockMvc mockMvc;
    private final JacksonTester<TorzsadatDto> torzsadatJson;

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Creating torzsadat with valid dto is successful.")
    public void testCreateTorzsadat() throws Exception {
        // given
        TorzsadatDto dto = TorzsadatDto.builder().azonosito("arany").megnevezes("arany").mertekegyseg("g").ar(100.0).build();

        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(post(BASE_URL)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
                .content(torzsadatJson.write(dto).getJson())
            )
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.azonosito", is("arany")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.megnevezes", is("arany")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.mertekegyseg", is("g")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.ar", is(100.0)));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Creating torzsadat fails validation when mandatory properties (azonosto, megnevezes, ar) are null, empty, or invalid.")
    public void testCreateTorzsadatFails() throws Exception {
        // given
        TorzsadatDto dto = TorzsadatDto.builder().azonosito("").megnevezes(null).mertekegyseg("  ").ar(-200.0).build();

        this.mockMvc.perform(post(BASE_URL)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
                .content(torzsadatJson.write(dto).getJson())
            )
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fieldErrors", hasSize(3)));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Creating torzsadat fails validation when azonosito not unique.")
    public void testCreateTorzsadatAdattipusokFails() throws Exception {
        // given
        TorzsadatDto dto = TorzsadatDto.builder().azonosito("au").megnevezes("arany 2").mertekegyseg("kg").ar(8000.0).build();

        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(post(BASE_URL)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
                .content(torzsadatJson.write(dto).getJson())
            )
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fieldErrors", hasSize(1)));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Get torzsadat request is successful.")
    public void testGetTorzsadat() throws Exception {
        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(get(GET_TORZSADAT_URL, 1))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id", is(1)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.azonosito", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.megnevezes", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.mertekegyseg", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.ar", notNullValue()));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Get all torzsadat request is successful.")
    public void testFindAllTorzsadat() throws Exception {
        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(get(BASE_URL))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$").isArray())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$", not(empty())));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Updating torzsadat with valid dto is successful.")
    public void testUpdateTorzsadat() throws Exception {
        // given
        TorzsadatDto dto = TorzsadatDto.builder().azonosito("e").megnevezes("euro").mertekegyseg("db").ar(420.0).build();

        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(put(GET_TORZSADAT_URL, 1)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
                .content(torzsadatJson.write(dto).getJson())
            )
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id", is(1)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.azonosito", is("e")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.megnevezes", is("euro")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.mertekegyseg", is("db")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.ar", is(420.0)));
    }
}

package hu.peterkepkeret.ajanlatkeszito.vevo;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.json.AutoConfigureJsonTesters;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.web.servlet.AutoConfigureMockMvc;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import org.springframework.boot.test.json.JacksonTester;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.LinkedMultiValueMap;
import org.springframework.util.MultiValueMap;
import hu.peterkepkeret.ajanlatkeszito.enums.VevoTipus;
import hu.peterkepkeret.ajanlatkeszito.vevo.dto.CimDto;
import hu.peterkepkeret.ajanlatkeszito.vevo.dto.VevoDto;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.empty;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.greaterThan;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.hasSize;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.is;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.not;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.notNullValue;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.nullValue;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.delete;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.put;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.content;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

@AutoConfigureMockMvc
@AutoConfigureJsonTesters
@SpringBootTest
@RequiredArgsConstructor(onConstructor_ = @Autowired)
@Transactional
public class VevoControllerIT {

    private static final String BASE_URL = "/api/vevo";
    private static final String GET_VEVO_URL = "/api/vevo/{id}";

    private final MockMvc mockMvc;
    private final JacksonTester<VevoDto> vevoJson;

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Creating vevo with valid dto is successful.")
    public void testCreateVevo() throws Exception {
        // given
        VevoDto dto = VevoDto.builder().vevoTipus(VevoTipus.MAGANSZEMELY).nev("Nagy Gábor").build();

        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(post(BASE_URL)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
                .content(vevoJson.write(dto).getJson())
            )
            .andExpect(status().isCreated())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.vevoTipus", is("MAGANSZEMELY")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.nev", is("Nagy Gábor")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cim", nullValue()));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Creating vevo fails validation when mandatory properties are null or empty.")
    public void testCreateVevoFails() throws Exception {
        // given
        VevoDto dto = VevoDto.builder().vevoTipus(null).nev(" ").engedmeny(1.1).build();

        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(post(BASE_URL)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
                .content(vevoJson.write(dto).getJson())
            )
            .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.fieldErrors", hasSize(3)));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Get vevo request is successful.")
    public void testGetVevo() throws Exception {
        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(get(GET_VEVO_URL, 1))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id", is(1)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.nev", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.vevoTipus", notNullValue()));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Find all vevo request is successful")
    public void testFindAllVevo() throws Exception {
        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(get(BASE_URL))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.content", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.content").isArray())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.content", not(empty())))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.totalPages", greaterThan(0)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.totalElements", greaterThan(0)));
    }

    @Test
    public void testFindAllWithTelepulesFilterIgnoreCase() throws Exception {
        // given
        MultiValueMap<String, String> params = new LinkedMultiValueMap<>();
        params.add("page", "0");
        params.add("size", "10");
        params.add("telepules", "eg");

        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(get(BASE_URL)
                .params(params))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.content", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.content").isArray())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.content", hasSize(1)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.totalPages", is(1)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.content[0].cim.telepules", is("Eger")));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("List all vevo request lists all AKTIV vevo")
    public void testListAllVevo() throws Exception {
        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(get(BASE_URL + "/list"))
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$").isArray())
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$", hasSize(4)));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Updating vevo with valid dto is successful.")
    public void testUpdateVevo() throws Exception {
        // given
        CimDto cimDto = CimDto.builder().iranyitoszam("1022").telepules("Budapest").utca("Deák 4.").build();
        VevoDto dto = VevoDto.builder().vevoTipus(VevoTipus.CEG).nev("Teszt Kft.").adoszam("12121212-3-20").engedmeny(0.1).cim(cimDto).build();

        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(put(GET_VEVO_URL, 1)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
                .content(vevoJson.write(dto).getJson())
            )
            .andExpect(status().isOk())
            .andExpect(content().contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.id", is(1)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.vevoTipus", is("CEG")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.nev", is("Teszt Kft.")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.adoszam", is("12121212-3-20")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.engedmeny", is(0.1)))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.telefonszam", nullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.email", nullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cim", notNullValue()))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cim.iranyitoszam", is("1022")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cim.telepules", is("Budapest")))
            .andExpect(jsonPath("$.cim.utca", is("Deák 4.")));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Deleting vevo is successful.")
    public void testDeleteVevo() throws Exception {
        // when, then
        this.mockMvc.perform(delete(GET_VEVO_URL, 1)
                .contentType((MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)))
            .andExpect(status().isOk());
    }
}
